- **v05-生产者vs消费者思维转换**: 用户发现自己总是站在消费者角度思考问题时；或想培养"像富人一样思考"的能力时；或发现自己的时间都花在消费而非创造上时。 ([R2h1/my-book-skills](https://github.com/R2h1/my-book-skills/tree/HEAD/%E7%99%BE%E4%B8%87%E5%AF%8C%E7%BF%81%E5%BF%AB%E8%BD%A6%E9%81%93/v05-%E7%94%9F%E4%BA%A7%E8%80%85vs%E6%B6%88%E8%B4%B9%E8%80%85%E6%80%9D%E7%BB%B4%E8%BD%AC%E6%8D%A2) · ★1)
- **v06-金钱引力与种子钱**: 用户想攒钱/存钱但觉得"攒不动、离目标太远"时；或想理解"为什么富人越富"的机制时；或想设定"第一桶金"目标并找到突破口时。关键信号："我什么时候才能攒到第一桶金"、"为什么钱越攒越没劲"、"富人是怎么滚雪球的"。不适用于具体投资产品选择。 ([R2h1/my-book-skills](https://github.com/R2h1/my-book-skills/tree/HEAD/%E9%87%91%E9%92%B1%E7%9A%84%E5%B1%9E%E6%80%A7%EF%BC%9A%E4%B8%80%E4%B8%AA%E5%AF%8C%E8%B1%AA%E7%9A%84%E8%B4%A2%E5%AF%8C%E7%A7%81%E6%88%BF%E8%AF%BE/v06-%E9%87%91%E9%92%B1%E5%BC%95%E5%8A%9B%E4%B8%8E%E7%A7%8D%E5%AD%90%E9%92%B1) · ★1)
- **v06-鹅与金蛋**: 当用户想开始储蓄但缺乏动力时激活。通过鹅和金蛋的比喻，让用户理解本金产生被动收入的原理。 ([R2h1/my-book-skills](https://github.com/R2h1/my-book-skills/tree/HEAD/%E5%B0%8F%E7%8B%97%E9%92%B1%E9%92%B1/v06-%E9%B9%85%E4%B8%8E%E9%87%91%E8%9B%8B) · ★1)
- **v07-收入分配法（50-40-10）**: 当用户发工资后不知道如何分配资金时激活。50%养鹅+40%梦想储蓄罐+10%零花的分配法。 ([R2h1/my-book-skills](https://github.com/R2h1/my-book-skills/tree/HEAD/%E5%B0%8F%E7%8B%97%E9%92%B1%E9%92%B1/v07-%E6%94%B6%E5%85%A5%E5%88%86%E9%85%8D%E6%B3%95%EF%BC%8850-40-10%EF%BC%89) · ★1)
- **v07-良债vs劣债**: 用户在考虑借钱/贷款（买房、创业、消费贷）时；或想评估自己现有债务是否健康时；或听到"有钱人都负债"而困惑时。关键信号："我该不该贷款买房/创业"、"负债是不是坏事"、"我的房贷算良债还是劣债"。不适用于"怎么还清债务"的具体账务处理（那是预算问题）。 ([R2h1/my-book-skills](https://github.com/R2h1/my-book-skills/tree/HEAD/%E9%87%91%E9%92%B1%E7%9A%84%E5%B1%9E%E6%80%A7%EF%BC%9A%E4%B8%80%E4%B8%AA%E5%AF%8C%E8%B1%AA%E7%9A%84%E8%B4%A2%E5%AF%8C%E7%A7%81%E6%88%BF%E8%AF%BE/v07-%E8%89%AF%E5%80%BAvs%E5%8A%A3%E5%80%BA) · ★1)
- **v08-债务处理四原则**: 当用户面临债务问题时激活。四个核心原则帮助摆脱债务恶性循环。 ([R2h1/my-book-skills](https://github.com/R2h1/my-book-skills/tree/HEAD/%E5%B0%8F%E7%8B%97%E9%92%B1%E9%92%B1/v08-%E5%80%BA%E5%8A%A1%E5%A4%84%E7%90%86%E5%9B%9B%E5%8E%9F%E5%88%99) · ★1)
- **v08-攒钱机制**: 用户总月光、攒不下钱时；或想建立"攒得住"的机制而不是靠意志力时；或想戒掉信用卡/冲动消费时。关键信号："我工资不低怎么还是月光"、"怎么才能真的存下钱"、"信用卡该不该留"。不适用于收入本身太低的问题（那是 v24 收入问题）。 ([R2h1/my-book-skills](https://github.com/R2h1/my-book-skills/tree/HEAD/%E9%87%91%E9%92%B1%E7%9A%84%E5%B1%9E%E6%80%A7%EF%BC%9A%E4%B8%80%E4%B8%AA%E5%AF%8C%E8%B1%AA%E7%9A%84%E8%B4%A2%E5%AF%8C%E7%A7%81%E6%88%BF%E8%AF%BE/v08-%E6%94%92%E9%92%B1%E6%9C%BA%E5%88%B6) · ★1)
- **v09-72公式（复利速算）**: 当用户需要估算投资收益或通货膨胀影响时激活。72公式快速计算本金翻倍或贬值年数。 ([R2h1/my-book-skills](https://github.com/R2h1/my-book-skills/tree/HEAD/%E5%B0%8F%E7%8B%97%E9%92%B1%E9%92%B1/v09-72%E5%85%AC%E5%BC%8F%EF%BC%88%E5%A4%8D%E5%88%A9%E9%80%9F%E7%AE%97%EF%BC%89) · ★1)
- **v09-风险与恐惧的逆向**: 用户面对市场暴跌/恐慌想割肉或抄底时；或看到身边人狂热追涨想判断风险时；或想建立"逆向但理性"的投资心态时。关键信号："股市崩了我要不要跑"、"现在是不是抄底时机"、"大家都在买是不是安全"。不适用于宏观行情预测（没人能预测）。 ([R2h1/my-book-skills](https://github.com/R2h1/my-book-skills/tree/HEAD/%E9%87%91%E9%92%B1%E7%9A%84%E5%B1%9E%E6%80%A7%EF%BC%9A%E4%B8%80%E4%B8%AA%E5%AF%8C%E8%B1%AA%E7%9A%84%E8%B4%A2%E5%AF%8C%E7%A7%81%E6%88%BF%E8%AF%BE/v09-%E9%A3%8E%E9%99%A9%E4%B8%8E%E6%81%90%E6%83%A7%E7%9A%84%E9%80%86%E5%90%91) · ★1)

- **v12x-agent-audit**: Auditoria de confiança de agentes, servidores MCP e skills — antes de instalar um de terceiro ou publicar o seu. Ferramenta determinística primeiro (extrai ferramentas, descrições, permissões e padrões perigosos sem rodar o servidor), verificação adversarial de cada achado, veredito de instalação/publicação. Use quando o usuário pedir "esse MCP é seguro?", "posso instalar essa skill/servidor?", "auditar meu agente", "meu agente vaza dado?", "checar tool poisoning", "revisar meu servidor MCP antes de publicar", "esse servidor é confiável?", "tem prompt injection no meu agente?", "agência exces… ([V12X/v12x-skills](https://github.com/V12X/v12x-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/v12x-agent-audit))
- **v12x-coherence**: Auditoria de coerência visual — SEM depender de um design system. Infere a régua que o próprio app já usa (alinhamento, espaçamento, raio, tipografia) a partir dos valores medidos e aponta onde o app se contradiz: desalinhamentos de 2-3px que o olho sente e não nomeia, espaçamento fora da própria escala, raios misturados, sprawl de tipografia. Mede geometria real no navegador (bounding boxes), separa defeito de decisão, e termina num veredito. Use quando o usuário pedir "conferir alinhamento tela a tela", "as telas estão desalinhadas", "espaçamento inconsistente", "unificar os cantos", "confe… ([V12X/v12x-skills](https://github.com/V12X/v12x-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/v12x-coherence))
- **v12x-design-audit**: Auditoria de conformidade ao design system, com aplicação das trocas. Varre tela a tela o que é realmente pintado, compara com os tokens (cor por ΔE perceptual, raio/espaço por distância, fonte por família), produz a tabela "valor usado → token" e aplica os swaps com verificação. Use quando o usuário pedir "aplicar o design system", "padronizar o layout", "as fontes não foram trocadas", "substituir uma cor específica", "o corner dos campos está errado", "varrer tela a tela", "checar conformidade de UI", "o protótipo não segue o design system", "auditar UI/UX", ou ao aplicar um design system d… ([V12X/v12x-skills](https://github.com/V12X/v12x-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/v12x-design-audit))
- **v12x-flow**: Scan de fluxo e usabilidade que OPERA o app, não opina sobre o print. Dirige o app tarefa por tarefa — clica, digita, completa o fluxo, exercita os estados esquecidos (vazio, carregando, erro) — e instrumenta console, rede e controles para achar o que está QUEBRADO (fato) antes do que é friccionado (fato-proxy) e do que é heurística (opinião rotulada). Web via navegador, nativo via simulador. Use quando o usuário pedir "scan de usabilidade", "o que não funciona no app", "UX complicada", "testar os fluxos", "conferir seção por seção", "o botão não faz nada", "revisar usabilidade do app e web",… ([V12X/v12x-skills](https://github.com/V12X/v12x-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/v12x-flow))
- **v12x-scan**: Auditoria de segurança em profundidade, com ferramentas determinísticas antes da análise por leitura, verificação adversarial de cada achado e relatório acionável. Use quando o usuário pedir "auditar segurança", "revisar segurança", "varredura de segurança", "tem vazamento?", "posso publicar isso?", "está seguro para open source?", "antes de publicar", "checar segredos", "auditar antes de entregar ao cliente", "hacker acessa pelo frontend", "checar IDOR/SSRF/XSS", "auditar meu agente/MCP", ou antes de tornar um repositório público, publicar app na loja, entregar código a terceiro, ou ligar mu… ([V12X/v12x-skills](https://github.com/V12X/v12x-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/v12x-scan))
- **v13-寄生性债务识别与清除**: 用户想重新审视自己的债务结构时；或考虑借贷消费/买房/买车时；或想理解"好债务"和"坏债务"的区别时。 ([R2h1/my-book-skills](https://github.com/R2h1/my-book-skills/tree/HEAD/%E7%99%BE%E4%B8%87%E5%AF%8C%E7%BF%81%E5%BF%AB%E8%BD%A6%E9%81%93/v13-%E5%AF%84%E7%94%9F%E6%80%A7%E5%80%BA%E5%8A%A1%E8%AF%86%E5%88%AB%E4%B8%8E%E6%B8%85%E9%99%A4) · ★1)

- **v15-专注所能**: 当用户说"我不行""没条件"时激活。把精力集中在自己能做的、知道的和拥有的东西上。 ([R2h1/my-book-skills](https://github.com/R2h1/my-book-skills/tree/HEAD/%E5%B0%8F%E7%8B%97%E9%92%B1%E9%92%B1/v15-%E4%B8%93%E6%B3%A8%E6%89%80%E8%83%BD) · ★1)
- **v15-长期持有**: 用户面对持有的资产波动、纠结"要不要卖"时；或想理解"为什么要长期持有、持有多久"时；或反思自己"总拿不住股票"时。关键信号："涨了要不要落袋"、"跌了要不要止损"、"怎么才能拿得住"。不适用于短线交易/择时（本书明确反对）。 ([R2h1/my-book-skills](https://github.com/R2h1/my-book-skills/tree/HEAD/%E9%87%91%E9%92%B1%E7%9A%84%E5%B1%9E%E6%80%A7%EF%BC%9A%E4%B8%80%E4%B8%AA%E5%AF%8C%E8%B1%AA%E7%9A%84%E8%B4%A2%E5%AF%8C%E7%A7%81%E6%88%BF%E8%AF%BE/v15-%E9%95%BF%E6%9C%9F%E6%8C%81%E6%9C%89) · ★1)
- **v16-慢变富与持续行动**: 用户焦虑"为什么还没财务自由/想快速致富"时；或想建立"长期主义+持续行动"的心态时；或想用"100天/3个月"的方法推动自己改变时。关键信号："我急着赚钱怎么办"、"怎么才能快点变富"、"我总是坚持不下来"。不适用于具体的投资操作。 ([R2h1/my-book-skills](https://github.com/R2h1/my-book-skills/tree/HEAD/%E9%87%91%E9%92%B1%E7%9A%84%E5%B1%9E%E6%80%A7%EF%BC%9A%E4%B8%80%E4%B8%AA%E5%AF%8C%E8%B1%AA%E7%9A%84%E8%B4%A2%E5%AF%8C%E7%A7%81%E6%88%BF%E8%AF%BE/v16-%E6%85%A2%E5%8F%98%E5%AF%8C%E4%B8%8E%E6%8C%81%E7%BB%AD%E8%A1%8C%E5%8A%A8) · ★1)
- **v16-量入为出**: 当用户觉得钱不够花但不知道花在哪里时激活。控制支出才能积累财富。 ([R2h1/my-book-skills](https://github.com/R2h1/my-book-skills/tree/HEAD/%E5%B0%8F%E7%8B%97%E9%92%B1%E9%92%B1/v16-%E9%87%8F%E5%85%A5%E4%B8%BA%E5%87%BA) · ★1)
- **v17-应对市场波动**: 当用户持有的基金或股票大跌时激活。理解市场周期，下跌时不恐慌卖出。 ([R2h1/my-book-skills](https://github.com/R2h1/my-book-skills/tree/HEAD/%E5%B0%8F%E7%8B%97%E9%92%B1%E9%92%B1/v17-%E5%BA%94%E5%AF%B9%E5%B8%82%E5%9C%BA%E6%B3%A2%E5%8A%A8) · ★1)
- **v17-独立判断**: 用户面对专家/大V/理财顾问的"内幕""预测"想判断是否可信时；或想建立"不依赖他人意见、自己学习做决定"的投资习惯时；或容易被权威/阴谋论带偏想保持清醒时。关键信号："这个专家说得对吗"、"大V推荐的能跟吗"、"为什么专家都预测不准"。不适用于获取基础金融知识本身。 ([R2h1/my-book-skills](https://github.com/R2h1/my-book-skills/tree/HEAD/%E9%87%91%E9%92%B1%E7%9A%84%E5%B1%9E%E6%80%A7%EF%BC%9A%E4%B8%80%E4%B8%AA%E5%AF%8C%E8%B1%AA%E7%9A%84%E8%B4%A2%E5%AF%8C%E7%A7%81%E6%88%BF%E8%AF%BE/v17-%E7%8B%AC%E7%AB%8B%E5%88%A4%E6%96%AD) · ★1)
- **v18-分散风险**: 当用户想All in某个投资产品时激活。不要把鸡蛋放在同一个篮子里。 ([R2h1/my-book-skills](https://github.com/R2h1/my-book-skills/tree/HEAD/%E5%B0%8F%E7%8B%97%E9%92%B1%E9%92%B1/v18-%E5%88%86%E6%95%A3%E9%A3%8E%E9%99%A9) · ★1)
- **v18-新闻vs投资信息**: 用户看到新闻标题（暴跌/失业/恐慌/暴涨）想判断"要不要据此操作"时；或想学会"看数据/比率而非被情绪标题绑架"时；或想识别媒体报道中的偏见时。关键信号："新闻说经济要完了要不要跑"、"这个数据是不是很吓人"、"媒体是不是在夸大"。不适用于获取具体新闻事实本身。 ([R2h1/my-book-skills](https://github.com/R2h1/my-book-skills/tree/HEAD/%E9%87%91%E9%92%B1%E7%9A%84%E5%B1%9E%E6%80%A7%EF%BC%9A%E4%B8%80%E4%B8%AA%E5%AF%8C%E8%B1%AA%E7%9A%84%E8%B4%A2%E5%AF%8C%E7%A7%81%E6%88%BF%E8%AF%BE/v18-%E6%96%B0%E9%97%BBvs%E6%8A%95%E8%B5%84%E4%BF%A1%E6%81%AF) · ★1)
- **v19-对待他人金钱**: 用户在面对公款/税金/AA/朋友请客/借用他人财物等场景，想判断"怎么对待别人的钱"时；或想培养"尊重他人金钱=尊重自己金钱"的品格时。关键信号："用公款/朋友的钱无所谓吧"、"聚餐是不是该点贵的"、"占小便宜没什么吧"。不适用于个人理财计算。 ([R2h1/my-book-skills](https://github.com/R2h1/my-book-skills/tree/HEAD/%E9%87%91%E9%92%B1%E7%9A%84%E5%B1%9E%E6%80%A7%EF%BC%9A%E4%B8%80%E4%B8%AA%E5%AF%8C%E8%B1%AA%E7%9A%84%E8%B4%A2%E5%AF%8C%E7%A7%81%E6%88%BF%E8%AF%BE/v19-%E5%AF%B9%E5%BE%85%E4%BB%96%E4%BA%BA%E9%87%91%E9%92%B1) · ★1)
- **v19-帮助他人与财富的意义**: 当用户赚钱后思考"然后呢"时激活。财富的意义在于有能力帮助他人。 ([R2h1/my-book-skills](https://github.com/R2h1/my-book-skills/tree/HEAD/%E5%B0%8F%E7%8B%97%E9%92%B1%E9%92%B1/v19-%E5%B8%AE%E5%8A%A9%E4%BB%96%E4%BA%BA%E4%B8%8E%E8%B4%A2%E5%AF%8C%E7%9A%84%E6%84%8F%E4%B9%89) · ★1)
- **v1b1-author**: Walk an LLM through a 5-step decision flow to scaffold a new PyLabRobot device under Rick Wierenga's v1b1 patterns. Generates a structurally-conformant Python scaffold + a markdown decision record. Calls the v1b1-capability review skill at hand-off as pre-emit lint stage 3. Sibling skill to v1b1-capability — reuses its reference.md and vocabulary-lockdown.md by path; never duplicates. ([vcjdeboer/v1b1-skills](https://github.com/vcjdeboer/v1b1-skills/tree/HEAD/v1b1-author))
- **v1b1-capability**: Review draft PyLabRobot Driver / CapabilityBackend / Capability / Device code against Rick Wierenga's v1b1 patterns and the principles those patterns embody. Produces a structured violation report with named principle, v1b1 evidence, and suggested fix per finding. Warns; never blocks. ([vcjdeboer/v1b1-skills](https://github.com/vcjdeboer/v1b1-skills/tree/HEAD/v1b1-capability))
- **v1b1-protocol**: Extract a structured command catalog from a vendor device's communication protocol (DLL, server API, or bytestream sniff) so the catalog drops cleanly into a v1b1-author scaffold's command-class slot. Third sibling to v1b1-capability (review) and v1b1-author (scaffold) — the trio covers the full PLR device-authoring lifecycle. Reads reference.md and vocabulary-lockdown.md from v1b1-capability by path; never duplicates. ([vcjdeboer/v1b1-skills](https://github.com/vcjdeboer/v1b1-skills/tree/HEAD/v1b1-protocol))
- **v1tals**: Checkup periódico de saúde de um repo — caça de dívida técnica COM conserto, e2e real no browser, gosto de design contra régua alta, ergonomia (5 stati), animações e performance; termina em fixes despachados, nunca em relatório de gaveta. Dispara a cada ~5 merges na main, antes de um gate, ou quando o dono pede um checkup. Não para um bug pontual, não para review de um único diff, e não substitui testes unitários. ([maxkle1nz/deviance-skills](https://github.com/maxkle1nz/deviance-skills/tree/HEAD/claude/v1tals) · ★2)
- **v2-1-80-demo-skill**: Demonstrates v2.1.80-plus features end to end - userConfig substitutions, CLAUDE_PLUGIN_OPTION env vars, and the v2.1.98 plugin-skill name field. Use when the user wants to see every Claude Code v2.1.80-plus feature exercised in one place. Trigger with phrases like "show v2.1.80 features", "demo userConfig", or "exercise the demo plugin". ([Emasoft/claude-plugins-validation](https://github.com/Emasoft/claude-plugins-validation/tree/HEAD/tests/fixtures/v2_1_80_plugin/skills/v2-1-80-demo-skill) · ★4)
- **v2-authoring**: Designing and building a NEW Bland v2 agent from scratch — scenario architecture (what scenarios represent and where boundaries belong), hub + entry authoring, step/edge/tool design, the standard conduct-rule set, and how v2 building differs from v1 pathway building. Use when creating a new v2 agent, designing its scenarios, or when someone asks how building in v2 differs from v1. If the user has not said v1 or v2, ASK before applying this doctrine. ([CINTELLILABS/bland-plugins](https://github.com/CINTELLILABS/bland-plugins/tree/HEAD/v2/skills/v2-authoring))
- **v2-lifecycle**: The Bland v2 agent lifecycle — environments (dev/staging/production), publish/promote/rollback, which version answers each channel at call time, branches and merging, A/B experiments, environment variables ({{env.KEY}}), pre-deploy checks, inbound number binding and the twin-pathway fallback. Use when shipping an agent version to real traffic, wiring numbers, gating a promotion, or answering "which version is this call running". ([CINTELLILABS/bland-plugins](https://github.com/CINTELLILABS/bland-plugins/tree/HEAD/v2/skills/v2-lifecycle))
- **v2-malicious**: "Bugfix release." Same friendly description. But the v1.0.1 "update" quietly added credential reads, a shell-out, and network egress that were not in the version you trusted. Diffing this against your v1 baseline is exactly how skillvet catches a rug-pull. ([cognis-digital/skillvet](https://github.com/cognis-digital/skillvet/tree/HEAD/demos/rugpull/v2-malicious))
- **v2-migration**: The hand-migration doctrine for converting a Bland v1 pathway or persona (raw JSON export) into a v2 agent snapshot — architecture rules, verbatim-carriage discipline, the full trap catalog, and the verification bar. Use whenever migrating or converting v1 pathways/personas to v2 agents, or when a migrated agent misbehaves. The /norm:migrate command drives this skill end to end. ([CINTELLILABS/bland-plugins](https://github.com/CINTELLILABS/bland-plugins/tree/HEAD/v2/skills/v2-migration))
- **v2-persona-slice**: This skill should be used when working in academy-manager and the user asks to add or change v2 backend routes, BFF APIs, DDD contexts, persona workflows, tenant isolation, SaaS paths, admin/coach/parent/platform interfaces, or migration-related backend/frontend slices. ([Ramc4685/academy-manager](https://github.com/Ramc4685/academy-manager/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/v2-persona-slice) · ★1)
- **v2-rest-api-backend**: Discover and install skills for AI agents. ([vercel-labs/skills](https://github.com/vercel-labs/skills) · ★32k)
- **v2-rest-api-frontend**: Discover and install skills for AI agents. ([vercel-labs/skills](https://github.com/vercel-labs/skills) · ★32k)
- **v2-runtime**: How a Bland v2 agent actually behaves at call time — the node routing decision stack (loop conditions, tool response pathways, deterministic edges, model choice), hub/scenario semantics, variable resolution, code-step execution, and the behavioral deltas every author must plan for. Use when predicting or debugging live agent behavior, explaining routing decisions, or answering "why did the call go there". ([CINTELLILABS/bland-plugins](https://github.com/CINTELLILABS/bland-plugins/tree/HEAD/v2/skills/v2-runtime))
- **v2-service-type**: 내 서비스 유형 결정하기. C2 PLAN 두 번째 스킬. Triggers: "v2 service-type", "v2-service-type", "서비스 유형", "유형 결정" ([daniel-kim-9way/learn-skills](https://github.com/daniel-kim-9way/learn-skills/tree/HEAD/plugins/vuild-learn-skills/skills/v2-service-type) · 4 copies)
- **v2-snapshot**: The Bland v2 agent snapshot dialect — the exact JSON shape of an agent version (behavior graph, hub, complex scenarios, flow steps, edges, tools, settings) and the rules for authoring one by hand. Use whenever reading, writing, or debugging an agent_versions snapshot, pushing a version via POST /v2/agents/:id/versions, or carrying content into a snapshot during migration. ([CINTELLILABS/bland-plugins](https://github.com/CINTELLILABS/bland-plugins/tree/HEAD/v2/skills/v2-snapshot))
- **v2-technical-star-rating**: A股 V2.50 技术评级技能。当前定位是“结构化技术分析报告”，不再把 V2 当作机械化交易信号系统。 复用 V2StockSelector、V2Timing 与主策略量能评级，输出 ★1-5 评级、ATR 自适应参考买入价、目标价、止损价、 风险收益比、参考仓位、量能评级和推荐理由。 支持 A 股 / 美股 / 港股三市场参数自动适配。 Use when the user asks for a structured V2 technical report, star-based rating, target/stop calculation, risk-reward analysis, or a human-in-the-loop technical assessment. ([herddeerman/stock-course-strategy-skill](https://github.com/herddeerman/stock-course-strategy-skill/tree/HEAD/v2-technical-rating) · ★4)
- **v2-testing**: Verifying a Bland v2 agent with platform simulations and test-chat probes — the agent-testing API contract, request-data fixtures, engine-trace grading, the test-chat WebSocket recipe, and testing-safety rules for live integrations. Use when setting up sims, grading results, running test chats, or deciding whether a test is safe to run at all. ([CINTELLILABS/bland-plugins](https://github.com/CINTELLILABS/bland-plugins/tree/HEAD/v2/skills/v2-testing))
- **v20-企业家精神**: 当用户想培养商业思维或孩子经济独立时激活。从小培养企业家素质和商业思维。 ([R2h1/my-book-skills](https://github.com/R2h1/my-book-skills/tree/HEAD/%E5%B0%8F%E7%8B%97%E9%92%B1%E9%92%B1/v20-%E4%BC%81%E4%B8%9A%E5%AE%B6%E7%B2%BE%E7%A5%9E) · ★1)
- **v20-消费观漂亮垃圾与不贪**: 用户面对"要不要买这件东西/这个奢侈消费"时；或被"高回报暴利项目"诱惑时；或想从"炫耀性消费"转向"需要性消费"时。关键信号："这个好漂亮想买"、"别人都买我也要买"、"三个月翻倍的项目要不要投"。不适用于投资标的选择（那是 v13/v14）。 ([R2h1/my-book-skills](https://github.com/R2h1/my-book-skills/tree/HEAD/%E9%87%91%E9%92%B1%E7%9A%84%E5%B1%9E%E6%80%A7%EF%BC%9A%E4%B8%80%E4%B8%AA%E5%AF%8C%E8%B1%AA%E7%9A%84%E8%B4%A2%E5%AF%8C%E7%A7%81%E6%88%BF%E8%AF%BE/v20-%E6%B6%88%E8%B4%B9%E8%A7%82%E6%BC%82%E4%BA%AE%E5%9E%83%E5%9C%BE%E4%B8%8E%E4%B8%8D%E8%B4%AA) · ★1)
- **v2026-3-migration**: Standardizes mobile UI components and backend routes to v2026.3 engineering standards (Zustand Selectors, FlashList, useMemo styling, and Zod validation). Use this when refactoring existing features or building new ones. ([hacka-tron/goalbuddy](https://github.com/hacka-tron/goalbuddy/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/v2026-3-migration/v2026-3-migration) · 2 copies)
- **v21-保险不是储蓄**: 用户被保险顾问推销"储蓄型/返本/养老/年金"保险时；或想判断"保险能不能当理财"时；或评估自己保费支出是否合理时。关键信号："这个保险能返本/当理财吗"、"保险是不是越早买越划算"、"我该买多少保险"。不适用于具体保险条款对比（那是专业咨询）。 ([R2h1/my-book-skills](https://github.com/R2h1/my-book-skills/tree/HEAD/%E9%87%91%E9%92%B1%E7%9A%84%E5%B1%9E%E6%80%A7%EF%BC%9A%E4%B8%80%E4%B8%AA%E5%AF%8C%E8%B1%AA%E7%9A%84%E8%B4%A2%E5%AF%8C%E7%A7%81%E6%88%BF%E8%AF%BE/v21-%E4%BF%9D%E9%99%A9%E4%B8%8D%E6%98%AF%E5%82%A8%E8%93%84) · ★1)
- **v22-免遭诈骗**: 用户遇到"高收益/内幕/稳赚/保本"的投资提议时；或想建立"拒绝过高收益、不碰不熟领域"的防骗纪律时；或反思自己为何容易被骗时。关键信号："这个项目稳赚不赔"、"内部渠道只赚不亏"、"你错过了就亏了"。不适用于识别具体骗局的取证（那找执法机构）。 ([R2h1/my-book-skills](https://github.com/R2h1/my-book-skills/tree/HEAD/%E9%87%91%E9%92%B1%E7%9A%84%E5%B1%9E%E6%80%A7%EF%BC%9A%E4%B8%80%E4%B8%AA%E5%AF%8C%E8%B1%AA%E7%9A%84%E8%B4%A2%E5%AF%8C%E7%A7%81%E6%88%BF%E8%AF%BE/v22-%E5%85%8D%E9%81%AD%E8%AF%88%E9%AA%97) · ★1)

- **v24-上班族与创业者变富路径**: 用户是上班族想变富、或纠结"要不要创业/进大企业还是小公司"时；或想用"一人公司"心态经营自己的职业生涯时。关键信号："上班族怎么才能变富"、"该不该辞职创业"、"去大公司还是小公司"。不适用于具体投资标的。 ([R2h1/my-book-skills](https://github.com/R2h1/my-book-skills/tree/HEAD/%E9%87%91%E9%92%B1%E7%9A%84%E5%B1%9E%E6%80%A7%EF%BC%9A%E4%B8%80%E4%B8%AA%E5%AF%8C%E8%B1%AA%E7%9A%84%E8%B4%A2%E5%AF%8C%E7%A7%81%E6%88%BF%E8%AF%BE/v24-%E4%B8%8A%E7%8F%AD%E6%97%8F%E4%B8%8E%E5%88%9B%E4%B8%9A%E8%80%85%E5%8F%98%E5%AF%8C%E8%B7%AF%E5%BE%84) · ★1)

- **v4-hook-delta-accounting**: Detect Uniswap V4 hooks that fail to settle currency deltas with the PoolManager. Every credit/debit a hook creates (BeforeSwapDelta, afterSwap hookDelta, take/mint, donate, settle/sync) is tracked in the manager's transient nonzeroDeltaCount; if the books aren't flat when unlock returns, the whole transaction reverts (CurrencyNotSettled), and mismatched take/settle/donate either strands hook funds in the manager or lets a swap leave with unpaid debt. Activate on hooks returning deltas, calling take/settle/mint/burn/donate, or custom unlockCallback accounting. ([iktok90-design/ai-smart-contract-auditor](https://github.com/iktok90-design/ai-smart-contract-auditor/tree/HEAD/rules/uniswap-v4-hooks/skills/v4-hook-delta-accounting) · ★37 · 2 copies)
- **v4-hook-generator**: Generate Uniswap v4 hook contracts via OpenZeppelin MCP. Use when building custom swap logic, async swaps, hook-owned liquidity, custom curves, dynamic fees, MEV protection, limit orders, or oracle hooks. ([Uniswap/uniswap-ai](https://github.com/Uniswap/uniswap-ai/tree/HEAD/packages/plugins/uniswap-hooks/skills/v4-hook-generator) · 585 installs)
- **v4-hook-permission-flags-mismatch**: Detect Uniswap V4 hooks whose address-encoded permission flags don't match the callbacks the hook actually implements. In V4 the hook's permissions live in the low bits of its deployed address (mined via CREATE2 salt) and must agree with getHookPermissions(); a callback the hook implements but whose flag bit is unset is never invoked, and a flag set without a real implementation makes pool initialization revert in Hooks.validateHookPermissions. Activate on any BaseHook/IHooks contract, getHookPermissions overrides, or hook address mining. ([iktok90-design/ai-smart-contract-auditor](https://github.com/iktok90-design/ai-smart-contract-auditor/tree/HEAD/rules/uniswap-v4-hooks/skills/v4-hook-permission-flags-mismatch) · ★37 · 2 copies)
- **v4-hook-reentrancy-via-unlock**: Detect reentrancy in Uniswap V4 hooks via the PoolManager unlock/lock callback. V4 uses a singleton PoolManager with transient lock state; all pool mutations happen inside an unlockCallback. A hook that makes external calls during beforeSwap/afterSwap/before*Liquidity (to tokens with hooks, arbitrary routers, or user-controlled contracts) can be re-entered, and because the manager is already unlocked the attacker can recursively swap/modify liquidity against stale hook state. Activate on any V4 hook performing external calls inside a callback, or custom unlockCallback logic. ([iktok90-design/ai-smart-contract-auditor](https://github.com/iktok90-design/ai-smart-contract-auditor/tree/HEAD/rules/uniswap-v4-hooks/skills/v4-hook-reentrancy-via-unlock) · ★37 · 2 copies)
